class="activity-body selectable line-clamp-6"><span><p>How to use HelpBnk</p><br>
<p>Our CEO <a rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank" class="text-sky-500" href="https://helpbnk.com/@adammoss">@adammoss</a> walks you round HelpBnk in under 4 minutes. Watch this first and you'll know where everything is.</p><br>
<p>Your first steps:</p><br>
<ol>
<li>
<p><a class="text-sky-500" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="https://helpbnk.com/connect/communities">Join your track's space</a>.</p>
</li><br>
<li>
<p>Every morning, open your space and watch the pinned video. It has your task for the day.</p>
</li><br>
<li>
<p>Add a photo to your profile so people can see who they're talking to.</p>
</li><br>
<li>
<p><a class="text-sky-500" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="https://www.simonsquibb.com/starter-kit?utm_source=wbbc">Apply for the Starter Kit</a>.</p>
</li>
</ol><br>
